AI 估值疑慮引發波動，惟股市仍有多頭可期
2026 年 7 月
全球主要股市 6 月 （截至24 日） 報酬跌多漲少，與5 月新興亞洲及 AI 半導體題材主導下的多頭
格局形成鮮明對比，主要因為市場同時承受美伊衝突升溫、 Fed 新主席華許上任後鷹派轉向，以及
AI 類股估值偏高引發的修正壓力，風險資產動能熄火。以美元計價6 月表現觀察，MSCI 中國與拉丁
美洲指數分別下跌 5.9%及 4.5%，表現最差。中國主因房市持續疲弱與內需復甦不均，並受其對中東
原油進口的高度依賴拖累 ， 拉丁美洲則受前波累積漲幅較大後的獲利了結與美元期間走強雙重壓抑。

美股因 AI 估值疑慮再起而拉回，拖累MSCI 新興市場與新興亞洲分別下跌 1.3%。惟有 MSCI 印度逆
勢上漲 1.9%，主要受惠 6 月下旬國際油價回落帶動。整體而言，6 月股市回檔較像是高基期後的短
線修正，並未改變今年資金偏好新興亞洲、日本與科技成長市場的方向。
6 月（截至 24 日）全球債市呈現「利率類承壓、信用類抗跌」的結構性分歧。全球主權債券單
月下跌 1.03%，跌幅居首，主因華許於 6 月會議釋出鷹派訊號，18 位官員中有 9 位於點陣圖計入今
年內至少一次升息，使美國 10 年期公債殖利率一度升至 4.5%，存續期間較長之利率類債券遭逢重
估。全球投資級公司債下跌 0.67%，跌幅次之，亦受存續期間拖累。新興當地債小跌 0.12%，反映
因美元指數回升使來自匯率的貢獻由正轉負。信用類債券則表現相對 較佳，新興美元債券單月上漲
0.53%、亞洲高收益債上漲 0.49%領先，主要受惠信用利差續處歷史低檔、企業基本面強健且違約率
仍偏低、到期牆延後且供給以再融資為主。

近期股市雖因 Fed 升息疑慮及 AI 類股估值過高而修正，但美光財報及蘋果商品漲價均印證 AI
產品供需緊俏且企業資本支出依然強勁 。 市場近期以類股輪動方式進行修正 ， 顯示投資人對於整體股
市信心仍在。在投資策略上，短期不宜追逐高估值個股，但可於震盪時分批布局 AI 基礎建設、半導
體供應鏈及台日股市。
債市方面，當前美債提供高於過往均值的高殖利率，將可相當程度抵銷殖利率續揚的風險。然因
投資人對 Fed 後續貨幣政策存有疑慮，或導致殖利率曲線短端受升息預期而有較大波動；長端又有
期限溢酬偏高、政府財政惡化及公債供給增加等風險。因此，建議債券佈局可聚焦 3 至 10 年期美國
公債及投等債，不僅能提供相對具吸引力的息收，若未來通膨預期放緩，在資本利得亦有表現機會。

美國股市：企業盈餘亮點集中 AI，股市評價尚屬合理，經濟成長趨緩但韌性猶存
美股第一季財報顯示，標普 500 成分股獲利有八成企業超越市場預期，整體每股盈餘同比增長
26.2%，創下 2021 年以來新高，企業盈餘上修動能強勁。隨運算需求從大模型訓練轉向推論，市場
資金快速轉向記憶體、光通訊、ASIC、CPU 與通用型伺服器供應鏈，引領板塊快速輪動。接下來第
二季 AI 產業仍將帶動資訊業獲利高增長，而能源、原物料產業第二季財報仍有不錯表現，但消費、
金融、醫療等，甚至通訊服務產業都有成長放緩跡象。
美國關稅談判正轉向以「301 法案」推進，目前針對「強迫勞動貨品進口禁令」，美國貿易代表

署（USTR） 已建議對全球60 個經濟體加徵 10%與 12.5%的額外關稅 。 預料下半年陸續將有更多301
調查結果公布，這將為川普關稅談判提供法源依據，迫使各國供應鏈加速赴美設廠，為美國製造業注
入投資動能，故對美國整體經濟表現無須看淡。
伴隨企業獲利預期上修 ， 標普500 指數前瞻本益比目前仍在 21.45 倍水準 ， 且通訊服務僅在20.9
倍、資訊科技僅在 24.8 倍，估值水平依舊合理。當前市場質疑重點在於AI 產業吸引大量資金投入資
本支出，故縮減企業發放現金股利與股票回購的能力，然而考量下半年仍有期中選舉行情可期，且
AI 產業變革快速，新題材層出不窮，操作上維持分批布局的建議。
美國經濟在 2026 年表現顯示成長趨緩但韌性猶存，2026 年第一季 GDP 成長率為 2.6%，表現
仍優於歐元區、日本等其他市場。失業率維持在低檔，5 月非農新增就業仍有 17.2 萬人，失業率持
平於 4.3%。5 月 CPI 年增率升至 4.3%， 核心CPI 年增率加速到 2.9%， 能源價格仍為推升通膨主因；

同月份 PPI 與核心 PPI 分別來到 6.5%與 4.9%， 顯示上游成本壓力正持續向下游轉嫁。勞動市場結構
性分歧，金融就業人數下滑，然而資料中心、半導體與電力等基礎建設投資，拉動非住宅營建業與製
造業僱傭需求，支撐整體就業數據，消費端方面，5 月美國零售銷售月增率回升至 0.9%，其中加油
站銷售與無店鋪零售商銷售(電商)表現強勁，前月走弱的汽車及零部件銷售逆勢反彈，顯示在K 型經
濟支撐下，高收入群體與特定消費仍表現穩健。
預料新任主席華許在確立聯準會改革方案前不急於升息，但目前利率仍處歷史高位，對房市、汽
車和消費造成壓抑 。 觀察密西根大學消費者信心指數在第二季來到有紀錄以來最低 ， 且美國新屋開工
下降，房市持續低迷，美國經濟仍需消化高利率與通膨帶來的負面效應。

歐洲股市：歐洲央行態度轉鷹，靜待美伊停火與關稅明朗化
歐洲央行在 6 月會議升息 1 碼，並持續警告中東地緣政治風險可能為通膨前景增添不確定性，因
此將今年通膨預期上調至 3.0%（前值 2.6%） ， 預計要到明年下半年才有機會回到 2% 的政策目標 。
不過，與 2022 年通膨高峰時期不同，目前歐洲就業市場並未出現明顯過熱，薪資壓力也未持續升
溫，因此我們認為本次升息後，歐洲央行更可能轉向觀望，利率期貨則顯示，市場仍預期年底前有再
升息 1 碼的可能性。
歐洲央行本次會議將邊際貸款利率、主要再融資利率及存款機制利率上調 1 碼至 2.65% 、

2.4% 與 2.25% ，會議中所有委員均同意升息。勞動力需求進一步降溫，薪資成長預計將在一年內
放緩，目前沒有看到與工資相關的二次效應，本次會議未討論中性利率和中性區間，如果不升息，通
膨因此失控 ， 重新控制將更加困難 。GDP 成長預估來看 ，2026 年爲 0.8%，2027 年爲 1.2%，2028
年爲 1.5% 。通膨方面，2026 年爲 3%，2027 年爲 2.3%，2028 年爲 2.0% 。核心通膨來看，
2026 年爲 2.5%，2027 年爲 2.5%，2028 年爲 2.2% 。
歐洲經濟成長動能放緩，但製造業維持擴張態勢，雖然歐洲央行將 2026、2027 年經濟成長率預
估值下調，自 0.9%、1.3%調整至 0.8%、1.2%。然 6 月歐元區綜合 PMI 自 48.5 回升至 49.5，顯示
景氣萎縮幅度正逐步收窄，製造業活動仍維持在擴張區間，同月服務業 PMI 雖僅 48.9，但也已釋出
跌勢趨緩訊號。
ECB 升息 1 碼對抗通膨風險，後續升息路徑分歧，受美伊衝突引發的油價影響，歐洲通膨壓力再

度上升，5 月歐元區 CPI 年增率已達 3.2%，為防範通膨失控，歐洲央行在 6 月利率決策會議將存款
利率從 2.0%上調至 2.25%。目前市場對歐洲央行後續的升息路徑看法顯著分歧，鷹派陣營認為 6 月
升息 1 碼後、第三季會再升 1 碼，並維持利率水平至 2027 年底；鴿派觀點認為隨油價回跌，短期內
再升息必要性降低，故後續仍將觀察各國通膨轉嫁能力。
待美伊戰事落幕、美歐關稅底定，歐股可望啟動估值修復，展望後市，若美伊停火協議落實、引
領國際油價回吐先前漲幅，將成為扭轉歐股當前弱勢、並吸引國際資金回流的催化劑。此外，美國改
採以 301 條款課徵關稅，對歐洲出口實質影響仍須觀察，惟預料美歐盟友關係穩固，終將透過談判
達成雙邊協議。目前泛歐 600 本益比以 2026 年獲利僅 15.6 倍。待短期不確定性因素陸續消退，市
場情緒可望改善，有利啟動估值修復行情。

日本市場：成長動能仰賴出口，政府補貼支撐景氣表現，股市屢創新高
GDP 動能放緩，受惠族群集中於AI，日本 2026 年第一季 GDP 年增率僅 0.4%，創下 2024 年走
出經濟衰退以來最疲弱表現，市場已將全年度 GDP 成長預估下調至 0.6%。然而 5 月出口年增來到
17%，創 2022 年 11 月以來最快增速，其中半導體相關出口年增逾六成、反映全球資料中心需求全
面爆發，推升記憶體晶片與電子零組件價格走揚，故受惠族群集中，下半年成長動能仍高度仰賴 AI
產業。
財政補貼壓低通膨 ， 油價回跌降低財政負擔，近期 ，首相高市早苗推行 「責任的積極財政」 政策，

透過實施電費、瓦斯補貼，並將全國汽油價格維持在每公升約170 日圓水平。未來隨補貼措施退場，
預期物價將出現階段性反彈。日本央行已於利率決策會議把本年度核心通膨預估上調至 2.8%。然而
在利率走高背景下，日本政府財政結構惡化，根據2026 財年政府預算規劃，國債利息支出將達總預
算的 10.65%，明顯高於前一財年的 9.13%。所幸美伊戰事有降溫跡象，預期日本政府將持續縮減刺
激方案規模。
日銀升息 1 碼，美日利差未收斂壓抑日圓表現，且日本央行於 6 月會議決定升息 1 碼至 1.0%，
主要考量國內物價上行風險加劇、薪資維持強勁成長動能，且經濟下行風險受控。市場普遍預期下次
升息落在 2026 年第四季。然而高市若為緩解財政壓力，可能干預日銀升息路徑。鑑於美日利差未出
現明顯收斂 ， 即使日本官方進場干預 ， 對日圓匯率的支撐效果恐怕相對有限 ， 日圓匯率預估於155-163
區間震盪。
日經指數近期屢創新高，6 月 22 日史上首度突破 72,000 點、連七個交易日刷新紀錄。上漲動能

來自日圓貶至 161 附近，顯著優化出口企業的盈餘表現，同時「實體 AI」投資計畫引爆半導體與 AI
類股行情 。 就2026 年以來 ， 領漲標的涵蓋東京威力科創 、 村田製作所 、 鎧俠 、 軟銀、 三菱UFJ 金融、
愛德萬測試、松下控股、三井住友金融、瑞穗金融、藤倉等。上漲族群主要為 AI 供應鏈以及受惠於
升息的金融族群，但即使過去一年日股飆升，目前以 2026 年獲利，本益比仍在 24 倍合理位置。
波克夏深化日本投資佈局，外資買盤同步加碼，波克夏對日本資產配置持續增加。自 2020 年對
伊藤忠、丸紅、三菱商事、三井物產、住友商事等五家各以略高於 5%持股起步，至 2026 年 5 月對
各家持股均已突破 10%， 顯示加碼日本仍是其核心策略 。 與此同時 ， 外資對日股買盤持續湧入 ，2026
年迄今(截至 6 月 20 日為止)外資買超日股的累計金額擴大至 10.2 兆日圓 ， 反映國際資本對日股後勢
依舊樂觀。

台灣股市：AI 需求支撐製造業 PMI 高檔擴張，暑假旺季與股市推升服務動能
當前臺灣經濟正處於生產擴張的黃金期。最新公布的總體經濟數據與半導體龍頭營收呈現全面共
振，印證全球 AI 資本支出（CapEx）並非流於故事題材，而是實質轉化為台廠的訂單與獲利。儘管
全球金融市場短期受到地緣政治干擾與美債殖利率波動影響 ， 引發台股在高檔震盪 ， 但主計總處大幅
上修 2026 年 GDP 預測至 9.64%，配合製造業 PMI 續擴張至 60.7%，顯示基本面強勁。我們認
為，台股高檔的回檔屬於健康的獲利了結與籌碼沉澱，長線多頭架構未變。建議採取拉回布局策略，

聚焦 AI 實體基礎設施與邊緣運算（Edge AI）雙主線。
國發會公布之 6 月製造業 PMI 報 60.7， 連續第 6 個月維持在 60.0%以上的擴張速度 ， 六大產業
中，僅食品暨紡織產業與交通工具回報緊縮。細項中，新增訂單與生產分別上揚至 60.4（前 59.6）
與 58.7（前 57.9） ，同時原物料存貨和客戶存貨分別回跌至 61.9（前 65.7）和 49.4（前 50.5），
顯示在 AI 伺服器、半導體與高階製造的強勁拉貨下，庫存水位仍屬可控。
5 月海關出口達 784.8 億美元（年增 51.7%） ，寫下連 31 紅紀錄。其中，資通訊產品出口年
增 75.2%，電子零組件出口創歷史新高。同時，5 月進口額創下 605.7 億美元歷史新高（年增
54.9%） ，核心物資多為半導體資本設備，依進口領先出口 1 至 2 季之歷史慣例，預示下半年出貨
動能將再創高峰。台積電 5 月合併營收達 4,169 億 7,500 萬元（年增 30.1%） 。先進製程與高階
封裝產能持續滿載，且透過定價調整順利維持毛利率在高檔，讓獲利能見度直接延伸至後續數年。

主計總處正式將 2026 全年經濟成長率（GDP）預測大幅上修 1.93 個百分點至 9.64%，創下
16 年來新高紀錄。第一季 GDP 初估更達 14.55%，創 48 年來單季新高。 總經結構分析顯示，
這波榮景是由「出口拉動投資、投資進而促進消費」的良性循環。全球主要雲端服務供應商（CSP）
調高資本支出，其新一代伺服器建置「必須向台灣採購」 。商品出口實質成長預估將帶動全年商品出
口總值達 8,945 億美元，其年增率創 50 年來最高水準，構成台灣 GDP 增長的關鍵引擎。
儘管台股指數在高檔震盪加劇，但在 9.64% 的 GDP 強勁基本面保護下，下檔支撐極度紮實。
隨著 COMPUTEX 後終端裝置陸續開出 ， 消費性電子將迎來升級週期 ， 可望讓下半年經濟加速升溫。
我們持續看多台灣股市。

中國股市：製造業需求回溫，然動能僅集中於高新技術產業與出口端
中國 6 月製造業需求回溫，動能集中新產業與出口端，製造業 PMI 為 50.3（前 50.0） ，較前
月回升，並連續四個月維持在擴張區間。主要動能來自新出口訂單回升至 50.1（前 48.6） ，以及新
訂單升至 51.2（前 49.9） ，皆較前月回升至擴張區間，顯示需求端及出口端表現亦有所回溫。生產
經營活動預期上升至 54.3（前 53.9） ，製造業前景保有一定韌性，細項觀察，6 月生產指數 51.4
（前 51.2） ，持續維持在擴張區間，本月在新訂單回升、庫存指數回落至 47.7（前 49.3）之下，新

訂單減庫存擴大至 3.5（前 0.6） 。 另外受到美伊衝突緩解影響 ， 主要原材料價格大幅回落至 54.2（前
60.5） ，外部壓力緩解。
產業方面，新舊動能延續上月分化格局。高技術製造業 PMI 升至 53.5（前 52.9） ，裝備製造業
升至 52.5（前 52.1） ，上升趨勢持續，相較之下，傳統高耗能產業 47.1（前 47.1） ，建築業商業活
動 49.0（前 48.8） ，仍維持在較弱格局。整體來看，美伊衝突緩解有效帶動中國製造廠商轉趨樂觀，
但多數仍集中在新產業及出口端，後續仍待觀察新產業能否有效帶動內需動能回升。
然而，內需數據尚未見回溫：中國第二季以來的消費、固定投資數據下滑，房地產未見起色。工
業生產年增長 4.5%，表現最為堅挺，主要受到政策性資本開支與科技硬需求的支撐。零售銷售5 月
單月年增率轉負， 即便有五一黃金周的短暫拉動，但節後服務消費迅速墜落顯示出居民消費依然保
守。固定資產投資年比下降 4.1% 。 這是過往作為壓艙石的投資首次跌破 4% 防線。民間固定資

產投資年比更劇烈下滑 7.1%。
整體來說，中國政府大規模刺激的可能性偏低，主要政策料是調降 LPR 或結構性定向降準。其
政策核心在於為中下游民營中小企業減輕債務壓力。現行溫和的企業設備更新與家電 「以舊換新」 政
策邊際效益遞減。為遏制消費滑入通縮螺旋，中央財政下半年將擴大赤字，直接補貼居民端，如發放
消費券、加大育兒養老津貼等等。中國總體經濟正處於重組期，缺乏向上突破的動能，但向下有國家
政策工具與新興硬科技的實質托底 。 房地產全面反轉或大眾消費復甦的機率偏低 。 雖政府政策扶植的
新科技產業仍有機會，但整體看法仍偏向保守。

印度股市：外部供給衝擊與內部基本面的對抗，看法中性
全球供應鏈受到中東戰事的實質干擾 ， 印度的前瞻景氣指標卻給市場帶來了巨大的驚喜 。 最新
公布的 2026 年 5 月 HSBC 印度綜合採購經理人指數由 4 月的 58.2 逆勢回升至 59.3，創下近
6 個月來的最高擴張紀錄。其中，服務業 PMI 由 58.8 顯著彈升至 59.8，主因是國內消費模式在
中東局勢干擾下成功「外轉內」 ，內需服務動能十分強勁；製造業 PMI 5 月最終值亦來到 55.0，高
於 4 月的 54.7。 雖然新出口訂單因中東衝突擴大而減速 ， 但國內基礎建設與製造業的 「預防性囤貨」

需求，成功支撐製造業維持在歷史平均水準之上。
然而，內需並無法完全沖刷外部供給側帶來的窒息感。印度高達 85% 的原油依賴進口，中東
戰事導致國際油價攀升至每桶近 120 美元，對印度財政與物價造成極大考驗。最新數據顯示，5 月
份消費者物價指數（CPI）年增率由 4 月的 3.5% 抬頭至 3.9%。這主要是受到汽車燃油價格凍結
政策結束的影響 。此外 ，比原油更長遠的隱憂來自於氣候 。 氣象機構預警有高機率出現乾旱 。 歷史上，
降雨不足與聖嬰現象會嚴重抑制糧食與作物產量 ， 豆類、 油籽等作物的受災風險顯著高於有灌溉保護
的水稻和小麥。彭博與野村等機構一致預警，這項氣候乾旱威脅將在 10 月農作物收成期引爆新一
輪食品通膨，預估將食品與飲料通膨推向 6.0%，並使下半年整體 CPI 衝破央行的容忍上限。
面對經常帳赤字擴大、國際收支（BOP）惡化與盧比貶值壓力，印度當局並未坐以待斃。首先
在貨幣政策方面，印度央行（RBI）於 6 月 5 日一致決議維持政策回購利率在 5.25% 不變，立場

維持「中性」 。央行認為盲目升息防禦匯率不僅無效，反而可能將能源衝擊轉化為內需重傷，因此決
策者選擇保留政策空間以依賴數據靈活決策。不過，經濟學家多數預期，隨著年底通膨風險逐漸壓過
成長擔憂，央行這項「按兵不動」的政策不會持續太久。 其次在財政工具方面，為保障佔人口半數
的農業人口並穩定農村經濟，政府宣布 2026/27 財年肥料補貼預算大增 20%，並階段性減免汽柴
油，支撐內需經濟。
印度市場目前正呈現明顯的「基本面強韌」與「評價面修正」的矛盾訊號。不可否認，5 月高
企的 PMI 與 1Q GDP 的暴衝證實其內部需求並未崩潰，而政府靈活的黃金關稅政策亦成功控制了
經常帳的惡化 。 然而 ， 中東戰事帶來的原油窒息感與下半年季風降雨不足 ， 是極難迴避的供給側利空 。
這兩項毒素預計將在第四季將通膨推向 6% 以上，進而逼迫央行轉為年底升息，更高的借貸成本屆
時將進一步抑制工業與內需需求，也讓股市有評價修正的壓力。因此，我們建議現階段應採取較為防
守的中長線投資策略，以在估值向下修正的震盪期中，鎖定長期結構性獲利。

拉美市場：巴西選舉風險與通膨預期升溫，後市應保守看待
6 月以來拉美市場主軸已由單純降息利多，轉為政治風險、通膨預期與央行動態三方拉扯。巴西
總統大選民調顯示魯拉領先幅度擴大，疊加巴西通膨預期與 Selic 利率路徑連續上修，使市場開始重
新評估 2026 年政策與財政不確定性，也使整體拉美股市表現持續承壓。
選舉方面，最新公布的 Genial/Quaest 民調指出，魯拉對波索納洛的領先幅度再度擴大，第一輪
由前次 39%對 33%，轉為本次 39%對 29%，魯拉領先差距升至 10 個百分點；第二輪亦由前次魯拉
42%對波索納洛 41%，轉為本次 44%對 38%，領先 6 個百分點。由於魯拉為較不受資本市場所青睞

的候選人，其當選可能性上升，預期將不利於巴西股市表現。
另一方面 ， 通膨預期惡化也使巴西降息交易持續降溫 。截至 6 月 15 日的巴西央行市場調查指出，
2026 年巴西通膨預期由 5.11%上修 19 個基點至 5.30%，持續高於 4.50%通膨目標區間上緣。2027
至 2029 年通膨預期亦全數高於 3.0%目標，顯示市場對通膨回到央行目標區間信心不足。
受此影響，市場對巴西政策利率路徑也持續轉鷹，2026、2027、2028 年底 Selic 利率預期分別
升至 13.75%、12.00%、10.25%，較前次的 13.50%、11.50%、10.00%再度上修。財政面同樣削弱
政策可信度，2026 至 2028 年基本財政餘額預期仍為 GDP 的-0.50%、-0.40%、-0.24%，名目財政
赤字 2026 至 2029 年亦維持超過 GDP 的 7%，顯示巴西財政赤字問題持續惡化。
貨幣政策方面，巴西央行 6 月 17 日全票決議降息 25 個基點，將 Selic 利率降至 14.25%，表面

上延續降息循環 ， 但其聲明與前瞻指引缺乏清晰度 ， 使市場更難判斷央行是否會對通膨預期重新升溫
採取足夠強硬態度。巴西央行通膨預測亦同步上修，2026 年底整體通膨預測由 4.6%升至 5.2%，自
由價格通膨預測在 2026 年第四季由 4.5%升至 5.3%，在通膨壓力持續升高的狀況下，巴西短期內進
一步降息空間已相當有限。
巴西以外拉美央行亦呈現類似政策兩難，智利央行 6 月 16 日宣布維持政策利率 4.5%不變 ， 即使
其 2026 年 GDP 成長預測降至 1%至 1.75%，但仍因油價與地緣風險推升短期通膨而選擇觀望。
展望後市，拉美市場在巴西大選風險升溫、通膨預期持續攀升的背景下，後市股市上行空間料將
受限，尤其全球股市近期受到 AI 題材帶動，拉美市場在缺乏成長敘事下，表現料將難以跟上全球指
數表現，故我們認為目前對拉美市場應保守看待。

科技類股：Token 經濟學與邊緣 AI 外溢效應，帶動科技股進一步走揚
當前全球科技股正處於由 AI 算力基礎設施引導的「結構性重新定價」新起點。首先半導體產值
將正式跨越破兆美元大關，市場對於「AI 泡沫破裂」的擔憂缺乏實質數據支持。超大型雲端服務商
（CSP） 具備強韌的資產負債表與 EBITDA 創造力，大舉發債並非盲目燒錢。「Token 經濟學」 打通
了算力消耗與商業變現的阻礙 ， 這些都是科技股的中長線利多。 雖然美國貨幣政策走向可能引發短線
估值震盪，但基本面依然偏多。
根據 IDC 最新報告，全球半導體市場規模正迎來歷史性的結構性跨越，2026 年全球半導體總營

收預估將達到 1.29 兆美元 ， 較 2025 年增 52.8%。 其中 ， 單是資料中心半導體營收就將達到 4,771
億美元，預計到 2030 年將達 8,432 億美元，獨佔市場半壁江山。2026 年全球記憶體營收暴增至
5,947 億美元，其中 DRAM 營收跳升至 4,186 億美元，年增率高達 177%。主因是 CSP 業者為
了搶奪 HBM 與高階 DDR 產能，願意支付極高溢價，推升整體板塊毛利率。
輝達執行長黃仁勳定調的 「Token 經濟學」 ，將技術敘事轉化為可量化的財務獲利模型，其核心
論點為 「Compute is revenue（運算就是收入） ，AI 工廠生產的實體商品就是 Token。 而產生Token
的數量就可以與資本開支互相匹配，進而由算力推估出資本開支的回報率。在 1GW 功耗限制下，
Blackwell 世代年營收約 300 億美元；進入次世代 Vera Rubin 晶片架構後，因運算密度拉升，相
同功耗下的年營收預估將暴增 5 倍至 1,500 億美元 ， 提供 CSP 業者持續加大資本支出的實質回報

率支持。
此外，隨著集中式雲端算力的建置跨過臨界點，AI 算力紅利正加速向終端裝置外溢。智慧型手機
與 PC 本地執行 AI 推論的需求已迎來全面爆發，Edge AI 正為半導體市場創造出全新的高價值需
求類別。邊緣 AI 擺脫了過往智慧終端僅比較出貨量的殺價競爭，轉向「高附加價值」的規格升級溢
價。長期而言，隨著 AI 代理（Agentic AI）在終端裝置的滲透率攀升，半導體將迎來新一波結構性
定價與穩健的增長動能。
短期來看，由於通膨數據具備黏性，市場正重新評估聯準會 （Fed） 貨幣政策再度升息的可能性。
此流動性緊縮預期直接推升了美債無風險利率 ，可能對高估值的科技權值股造成本益比階段性震盪修
正。然而，科技巨頭極其強韌的實質獲利能力足以消化估值衝擊。2026 年半導體跨越 1.29 兆美元
產值已成剛需，CSP 業者 EBITDA 預估增長 13%，配合黃仁勳 Token 經濟學的變現邏輯，獲利增
長將有效消化高本益比，建議可持續加碼科技類股。

### Page 10

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
能源類股：荷莫茲海峽運量逐步恢復，油價料將承壓
受到美伊戰爭持續朝收斂方向前進影響，市場重新評估波斯灣出口恢復後的供給回補壓力；同
時，近期中國原油需求與進口動能明顯轉弱，使油市主軸由短線斷供風險，轉向中期供給過剩與需求
破壞，受此影響，6 月油價出現大幅回落，相關能源類股表現也出現承壓。
高盛報告指出，自美伊 6 月 17 日達成臨時協議後，荷莫茲海峽與波斯灣出口流量快速回升，中
東局勢於 6 月 27 日油輪襲擊後再度升溫前，波斯灣石油流量七日均值已達每日 1,800 萬桶，約為戰
前水準近 80%。其中荷莫茲海峽流量在協議後前 10 日增加每日 700 萬桶，6 月 24 日單日估算流量

一度達每日 1,300 萬桶 ， 約為正常水準60%； 若依過去兩週平均每日增加100 萬桶的恢復速度推估，
波斯灣流量最快可在 7 月初回到戰前每日 2,300 萬桶水準。不過，6 月 27 日油輪襲擊後，多艘超大
型油輪傳出掉頭返航 ，6 月 27 日至 6 月 28 日前後經荷莫茲海峽出口量一度降至每日 500 萬桶以下 ，
不到正常水準 20%， 顯示中東石油出口恢復方向雖明確 ， 但短期內仍取決於船東對海峽安全的信心。
另一方面，需求端壓力也有望加速全球石油供需平衡。IEA（國際能源署）6 月月報估計，第二
季全球石油供應缺口為每日 310 萬桶 ， 低於高盛估計的每日 500 萬桶 ， 主因 IEA 對石油需求破壞
的估計更高，IEA 預估第二季需求破壞量為每日 550 萬桶，高於高盛估計的每日 490 萬桶。需求
端疲弱原因主要來自中國需求下滑。彭博分析指出，中國 5 月原油需求急劇下降，原油進口量降至
每日 680 萬桶，較去年同期下滑 40%，主因包含荷莫茲海峽關閉期間造成的採購干擾，以及經濟

放緩帶來的需求破壞。進一步看至 2027 年，IEA 預估在供給年增每日 790 萬桶，以及部分需求疲
弱延續下，全球每日將出現 500 萬桶供給過剩，高於高盛預估的每日 320 萬桶，顯示中期油市風
險已由短缺轉向過剩。
受到整體油市轉往供給過剩影響，高盛將 2026 年第四季布蘭特原油價格預測由每桶 90 美元下
修至 80 美元，2027 年平均價格由 80 美元下修至 75 美元；西德州原油方面，2026 年第四季均價
預估由 85 美元下修至 75 美元，2027 年則由 75 美元下修至 70 美元。摩根大通、巴克萊、摩根士
丹利、瑞銀與滙豐也紛紛下修今明兩年油價展望，預估 2027 年油價均價介於每桶 63 至 85 美元；
其中巴克萊預期第四季可能出現每日 300 萬桶供應過剩，滙豐則預期短期將出現小型供應過剩。
展望後市，能源類股仍需觀察美伊臨時備忘錄落實狀況、荷莫茲海峽運輸恢復及中東增產狀況是
否順暢，然而當前市場更為關注停戰帶來的風險溢價收斂，故中短期內油價表現料將較為疲軟，在此

狀況下 ， 能源類股表現料將難以勝過由 AI 題材帶動的全球指數表現 ，故對後市我們亦維持保守看法。

### Page 11

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
黃金類股：央行增持黃金趨勢未變，惟聯準會偏鷹不利金價表現
6 月以來黃金類股表現持續落後整體指數表現，主因受到美國聯準會態度偏鷹派影響，市場升息
預期上升，推升美元與美債殖利率走強，連帶提高黃金這類無孳息資產的機會成本，使金價與黃金類
股短線表現持續承壓。
6 月 FOMC 會議雖維持聯邦基準利率在 3.5%至 3.75%不變，但利率點陣圖中位數由 3 月時預估
2026 年底的 3.375%上修至 3.75%，同時 18 位提交預測委員中，有 9 位預期年內至少升息一次，8
位預期維持不變，僅 1 位預期降息 1 碼，導致會後市場升息預期明顯攀升。對黃金而言，升息預期

升溫將推升美元與美債殖利率 ， 並提高持有無息資產的機會成本 ， 尤其金價先前在美伊戰爭導致長天
期公債殖利率攀升的影響下，表現已相對疲弱，近期利率預期轉鷹更容易使短線獲利了結壓力升高。
另一方面，新任聯準會主席 Kevin Warsh 在本次會議中並未屈從政治面的降息壓力，反而重申
2%通膨目標並強調貨幣政策的獨立性，有助於強化市場對聯準會政策紀律的信心。此舉雖有助於提
升美國政策可信度，卻也將對黃金帶來不利影響；由於黃金的部分避險買盤，源自於市場對美國財政
負擔、政策獨立性及美元信用的疑慮，故一旦市場相信聯準會將維持堅定對抗通膨的立場，這類避險
需求便可能逐步減弱，也讓黃金類股失去美國政策性避險這項題材。
然而，儘管短線黃金逆風重重，但是在央行買盤方面，仍未見到全球央行對於增持黃金的看法有
所轉變。世界黃金協會所出版的《2026 年央行黃金儲備調查》顯示，89%受訪央行預期未來 12 個
月全球央行黃金儲備將增加 ， 雖低於2025 年的 95%， 但仍高於2024 年的 81%與 2023 年的 71%，

顯示多數國家央行對於持續增持黃金儲備仍具高度共識。就自身配置而言， 45%受訪央行預期未來
12 個月自身黃金儲備將增加，高於 2025 年的 43%與 2024 年的 29%，並創下 2019 年以來最高比
例，另有 54%預期維持不變，僅 1%預期下降，顯示各國央行黃金配置意願尚未出現明確轉向 態勢。
該項調查也指出，各國央行增持黃金的原因，主要仍集中在儲備多元化與風險分散。90%受訪央
行認為黃金在危機時期具有避險性 ，84%認為黃金具長期保值與通膨避險功能 ，83%認為黃金是有效
的投資組合分散工具，80%認為黃金屬於儲備多元化政策的一部分。由此可見，央行買金並非單純追
逐短線價格，而是與官方儲備安全性、幣別分散及危機應對需求有關。
展望後市，全球央行增持黃金的趨勢仍未改變，然而短線上，在降息利多消失、美元及美債殖利
率偏向上行，且聯準會獨立性疑慮降溫的背景下，金價表現料將受到壓抑，預期後市黃金類股表現將
於區間內震盪整理，目前對黃金類股後市中性看待。

### Page 12

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：市場觀望華許首秀，美債利率料陷整理走勢
6 月中的 FOMC 會議以 12–0 一致決議維持聯邦資金利率目標區間於 3.50%–3.75%， 並重申維
持充足準備金政策。然而，投資人所真正關注的內容在於會後所公布的經濟預測摘要(SEP)。在 6 月
點陣圖中，九位官員預計今年至少會升息 1 碼，其中六位預計至少升息 2 次。另有九位官員預計利
率維持不變或會降息 。2026 年底聯邦基金利率中位數由 3 月的 3.4% 上修到 3.8%，2027 年由 3.1%
上修到 3.6%；同時，2026 年 PCE 通膨預估由 2.7% 大幅上修至 3.6%，核心 PCE 由 2.7% 上修

至 3.3%。這代表 Fed 不只承認通膨反彈，也認為利率路徑需要重新往上定價。彭博對於 Fed 主席
開場談話的 NLP 情緒模型亦反映了鷹派傾向的變化。
華許在此次的會後記者會中雖重申聯準會對物價穩定與最大化就業雙重使命的承諾 ， 但針對已持
續五年多高於 2%目標的通膨問題，他強調委員會將毫不含糊地致力於恢復物價穩定。同時Fed 也將
維持銀行系統的充足準備金政策。他也指出，儘管面臨不確定性，目前經濟活動穩步擴張，生產力和
資本投資強勁，且就業市場保持穩定；本次會議發布了更簡短直接的政策聲明，取消了華許認為不合
時宜的 「前瞻性指引」 ，使得此次會後聲明用字大幅精簡 （由4/29 會議時的 341 字減至 130 字） ，
Warsh 本人也選擇不參與提交經濟預測摘要（SEP）。作為領導層上任後的重要革新，他也宣布在未
來兩週內正式成立五個獨立小組以分別對「溝通」、「資產負債表」、「數據來源」、「生產力／就
業」與「通膨框架」等議題進行改革，旨在打造一個適應時代需求且專注於未來的聯準會。

美國總體經濟環境現處於「經濟具韌性但通膨僵固」的微妙平衡。即便 FOMC 6 月會議立場偏
向鷹派，但仍約有半數委員認為年底前利率將維持現行水準。彭博指出，倘美伊停火協議得以維持，
通膨或於未來數月回落，部分 6 月預期升息的委員 9 月可望轉為按兵不動。元大投顧認為「年底前
維持利率不變仍是大概率事件」 ，因近期震盪部分是因市場「尚未適應 Warsh 新溝通風格」所產生
的過度反應。此外，高盛在 FOMC 會後發表的最新報告也指出即便  Fed「升息的可能性雖增加，
但仍非其基本預期」。
我們認為，當前美債已充分反映通膨上行風險，並提供高於過往均值的高殖利率，將可相當程度
抵銷殖利率進一步走高之風險。然因投資人對 Fed 的後續貨幣政策存有疑慮，或導致殖利率曲線短
端受升息預期變化而有較大波動；長端又有期限溢酬偏高、政府財政惡化及公債供給增加等風險，故
存續期過長亦非明智之舉。因此，建議債券佈局可聚焦 3 至 10 年期美國公債及投等債，不僅能提供
相對具吸引力的息收，若未來通膨預期隨中東緊張情勢放緩，在資本利得方面亦可望有所表現。

2026 年 10 月 30 日

調査レポート

2026 年 7～9 月期の GDP（1 次速報）予測

～ほぼゼロ成長も、緩やかな景気回復は継続～

調査部 主任研究員 藤田 隼平

2026 年 11 月 16 日に内閣府が公表する 7～9 月期の実質 GDP（1 次速報値）は、前期比＋0.0%（前期比年
率換算＋0.1%）と予測する。設備投資や政府消費の増加に支えられ、かろうじて 4 四半期連続のプラス成長を
維持したものの、個人消費や輸出が前期の一時的な押し上げ要因の剥落もあって減少したことで、ほぼゼロ成長
にとどまったと見込まれる。なお、輸入が鉱物性燃料を中心に持ち直したことで外需寄与度は大きめのマイナスと
なるものの、公的在庫のプラス寄与により、おおむね相殺されたと考えられる。 
また、名目 GDP は、前期比＋0.9%（年率換算＋3.8%）となったと見込まれる。コスト増加分の価格転嫁が進
む中で、GDP デフレーターは前年比＋2.7%（季節調整済前期比では＋0.9%）と高めの伸びが続いた。 
今回、7～9 月期の実質 GDP はほぼゼロ成長にとどまる見込みだが、イラン情勢の悪化という逆風を踏まえれ
ば、国内景気は底堅さを維持している 。先行きについては、輸入コストの高止まりに伴う価格転嫁の進展が景気
の重しとなる可能性はあるものの、良好な雇用・所得環境や企業業績の改善を背景に内需の回復基調が維持さ
れることなどから、国内景気は緩やかな回復を続けると期待される。

（注）内需寄与度、民間在庫、外需寄与度は実質 GDP に対する寄与度 
（出所）内閣府「四半期別 GDP 速報」により作成

### Page 2

2 / 2

需要項目別の動向は以下の通りである。

① 実質個人消費（前期比 －0.1%） 
 良好な所得環境が維持され る中、消費者マインドも持ち直しへ向かったものの、前期に 耐久財
が自動車やエアコンを中心に 一時的な要因によって増加した反動 が生じた とみられる 。価格転
嫁の進展による 物価上昇 、地震や台風といった自然災害の発生 も重しとなった 可能性がある 。 
② 実質住宅投資（前期比 －0.3%） 
 住宅投資に先行する住宅着工戸数 が横ばいとなる中 、建築コストが上昇していることもあり、 住
宅投資は小幅ながら減少が続いたとみられる 。 
③ 実質設備投資（前期比 ＋0.9%） 
 企業の投資意欲は 省力化やデジタル需要 などに支えられて 堅調であり 、設備投資は 3 四半期
ぶりに増加 したと見込まれる 。機械設備投資の供給側統計である資本財総供給（除く輸送機械）
も 7～9 月期は前期比 ＋5.3%と増加した。 
④ 実質民間在庫（前期比寄与度 －0.0%ポイント ） 
 前期に 供給制約懸念の高まりを受けて、メーカーが製品在庫などの積み増しを進めた 反動もあ
り、7～9 月期はマイナス 寄与になったと考えられる 。 
 なお、公的在庫 について は、政府による代替調達の進展により 石油備蓄の 減少ペースが大きく鈍
化したことから、前期比寄与度 ＋0.5% ポイントと プラス寄与になったと みられる 。 
⑤ 実質政府最終消費支出（前期比＋ 0.3%） 
 高齢化が進む中、医療費 ・介護費を中心に 底堅く推移 したと予測する。  
⑥ 実質公共投資（前期比 －2.7%） 
 建築・土木コストの上昇が続いている中、 7～8 月の建設総合統計（出来高ベース：公共） は弱
めの動きとなった ことから 、実質公共投資は 2 四半期 連続で 減少したと見込まれる。  
⑦ 外需（前期比寄与度 －0.5%ポイント 、実質輸出：前期比 －0.5%、実質輸入：同 ＋1.7%） 
 輸出は 前期比 －0.5%と減少したと予測する。財は 堅調な 米国向け や中東向けの持ち直しを受
けて増加 した一方、サービスは 前期に 研究開発サービスが一時 的な 要因により上振れた 反動
で減少 したとみられる。  
 輸入 は前期比 ＋1.7%と増加したと 予測する。 特に財が鉱物性燃料 を中心に 持ち直した。ただ
し、サービス については 減少した 可能性がある 。 
 結果 、輸出から輸入を差し引いた 外需 の寄与度は －0.5%ポイント とマイナス になった と見込ま
れる。

• Hon Hai's partnership with NVIDIA is extending across GPU, CPUs, LPU, 
DPUs, networking and integrated compute systems. Management 
highlighted more than ten dedicated manufacturing facilities supporting 
Vera Rubin production, underscoring the scale of its preparation. A notable 
engineering breakthrough is the redesign of compute trays to eliminate 
conventional cables, liquid-cooling hoses and fans, simplifying assembly, 
improving reliability and enabling greater automation. Hon Hai also 
showcased its work on robotic assembly, leveraging Omniverse digital 
twins, computer vision and physical AI models to automate complex 
operations previously requiring manual precision. These include busbar 
installation and high-density connector insertion. We believe deeper 
design-for-manufacturing and design-for-automation capabilities should 
help Hon Hai accelerate production ramps, improve manufacturing yields 
and as AI systems become increasingly complex. We also believe this will be 
the core competence for Hon Hai to secure next-generation rack-scale 
systems, where manufacturing complexity and system-level validation 
increasingly differentiate suppliers.
• NVIDIA DSX POD, a factory-integrated, pre-validated modular AI 
infrastructure solution, provided by Hon Hai. Each POD contains seven 
Vera Rubin NVL72 and three Spectrum-6 SPX racks, and incorporating 
more than 10 million components. By completing rack integration, cabling, 
liquid-cooling connections and functional testing before shipment, the 
solution aims to reduce onsite deployment from approximately six months 
to as little as 14 days. NVIDIA expects its partner ecosystem to deliver 
these solutions beginning in early 2027. Hon Hai is also collaborating with

TECO on modular data centers that integrate IT systems, power distribution and cooling within 40-foot containers,
with more than 80% of modular assembly completed at the factory. We believe this model could meaningfully
expand Hon Hai’s revenue opportunity beyond rack assembly into fully integrated infrastructure, while accelerating
time to first revenue-generating AI tokens.
• Hon Hai highlighted its expanding role in next-generation AI networking, including NVIDIA’s Spectrum-6 SN6800

CPO switch, delivering 409.6Tbps aggregate bandwidth through 512 ports at 800Gbps. Hon Hai showcased
capabilities spanning CPO optical components, ELSFP modules, high-speed connectivity and complete switch-tray
integration, positioning itself to capture additional networking content as optical interconnect adoption accelerates.
Next, we expect the scale-up CPO with NVL576 to be announced with VRU next year; Hon Hai will remain the sole
supplier, and in our view, its first-mover advantage will be supported for further revenue and earnings upside.

• Other than VR200 system, Hon Hai also demonstrated Nvidia's LPUs, integrated into its Vera Rubin ecosystem
through LPX inference racks containing 256 LPUs. Unlike GPUs optimized for parallel computation, LPUs
accelerate latency-sensitive token generation, particularly feed-forward and mixture-of-experts execution,
complementing Rubin GPUs handling prefill and attention workloads. We believe the heterogeneous GPU-LPU
computing could expand Hon Hai's addressable market in AI infrastructure integration.

• Beyond AI Data Centers: Hon Hai highlighted progress in EV, autonomous mobility, robotics and smart healthcare.  In
EVs, it introduced the affordable Model A multi-purpose vehicle, targeting Japan and Southeast Asia. Its CDMS
strategy focuses on shortening development cycles and improving cost competitiveness. In robotics, Hon Hai
demonstrated physical AI, humanoid robots and digital-twin-enabled manufacturing automation, supported by its
collaboration with NVIDIA and ABB Robotics.  The company also showcased emerging capabilities in SiC

semiconductors, low-Earth-orbit satellites and FoxBrain AI models. We believe these initiatives reinforce Hon Hai's
longer-term transformation into a diversified technology manufacturing platform.

Hon Hai Precision
Valuation
Our target price of NT$400 is based on 18x average 2026E-27E EPS, which is at the higher end of the historical trading range.
We believe this multiple is justified by the company’s enhanced sales growth and profit margin outlook, as well as new
endeavors into AI server, EV, digital health and robotics. Hon Hai has been able to generate positive earnings streams, and thus

we believe a P/E valuation methodology is appropriate.
Risks
Downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price include: 1) slower-than-expected iPhone demand;
2) intensified peer competition; 3) weaker-than-expected execution on new business progress; and 4) higher-than-expected
impact of COVID-19 or other macro factors.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and

Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Airtac
Valuation
We derive our target price of NT$1,430 for Airtac by using a P/E methodology, as we believe the margin and earnings growth
outlooks will be the key drivers of the stock. We apply a P/E of 21.5x for 2027E, which is at par with its historical average.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the Airtac stock from reaching our target price include: 1) a softer-than-expected China
macro economy, resulting in downside to our sales forecasts; 2) intensifying competition leading to gross margin pressures;

and 3) slower-than-expected progress of its linear guide business, causing low utilization of capacity.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

2026/11/04 投資速報

環球晶(6488.TT) 
短期獲利受火災影響，但供需吃緊及漲價趨勢不變

營收走勢圖

研究員/鄭高輯 OWENCHENG@uni-psg.com

結論: 
環球晶短期獲利表現低於預期，主要原因包括: (1) 義大利 Novara
廠 7 月火災影響、(2)短期產能受限，以及(3)2H26 仍適用舊LTA 價
格 。 不過最壞狀況已過 ， 隨著時序進入4Q26， 預期營收將優於3Q26，
且自 1Q27 起 ， 隨著新產能開出 ， 以及開始適用價格調漲後的新LTA，
獲利表現將明顯轉佳 。 預估2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為17.82 元
(原估 19.49 元)、33.14 元(原估 32.53 元)。在投資建議上，儘管
短期營運不如預期 ， 但環球晶股價已大幅修正 ， 且2027 年半導體及
記憶體新 Fab 產能開出的趨勢明確(13 座->23 座)， 因此矽晶圓供需
轉緊且價格上漲的趨勢不變， 故維持買進建議 ， 然考量近期AI 供應
鏈出貨不順的雜音較多 ，漲價概念股評價受壓抑 ， 故將PBR 由 6X 調
整至 5X (PBR 歷史區間 2~6X，漲價循環合理評價應在中上緣 4~6X
之間)，將目標價由 1350 元下調至 1170 元。

重點摘要:

1.3Q26 財報表現不佳，主要受義大利 Novara 廠火災影響:  
3Q26 營收-2.3%QoQ(+2.6%YoY)，若排除 7 月義大利 Novara 廠火災
之影響，營收原本可以成長雙位數%QoQ。在毛利率方面，儘管3Q26
折舊費用略較前季下降 ， 但因Novara 火災導致部分高毛利率的 8 吋
SOI 基板出貨中斷 ， 導致毛利率較前季下滑0.6pt 至 20%， 不過在費
用控管下，OPM 較前季回升 0.4pt 至 9.8%。單季稅後 EPS 3.13 元，
低於原預期的 3.64 元 ， 且明顯低於前季的7.9 元 ， 差異主要係2Q26
認列較多 WAF 金融評價利益。

2.美光支付 5 億美元並非是預付款，而是認購 CB:  
今年 7 月環球晶與美光宣布簽訂 10 年期 LTA，美光將支付環球晶 5
億美元，然而，並非以預付款形式支付，而是環球晶發行上限 5 億
美元的可轉債，由美光擔任唯一應募人。研究員認為，客戶支付預
付款綁定產能的強制力相對較強 (預付款在出貨後會轉營收且不退
還)，然而，CB 存在股本稀釋的問題(推估約 3%上下)，相對較不受
歡迎。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3.2027 年起開始適用新 LTA 價格，ASP 將改善:  
在價格趨勢方面，公司對於價格調整將採取逐案談判 (Case-by-
case)，依據產品規格、供需狀況、合約到期時間與個別客戶情況進
行協議，非全產品線統一調漲特定百分比。公司認為目前市場環境
日益支持價格回歸合理水準，且已有多筆新 LTA 接近洽談完成，預
計自 2027 年起整體 ASP 將受惠於新 LTA 與產品組合優化而有所改
善。

3.4Q26 營收會優於 3Q26，但受限產能，且價格仍沿用舊合約:  
展望 4Q26，義大利 Novara 廠已正式啟動部分復工，且整體客戶需
求動能依然非常強勁 ， 甚至有客戶願意提前適用新價格以爭取產能 ，
但受限於產能不足 ， 且4Q26 絕大多數價格仍適用舊合約 ， 因此預期
4Q26 營收會優於 3Q26， 但仍無法達成雙位數%成長 。 不過自1Q27 起，
隨著新產能開出以及 ASP 提升，營運動能將轉佳。研究員預估2026
年營收-1.0%YoY，稅後EPS 17.82 元(原估 19.49 元，下修主要是因
為火災)。

4.2027 年營運將明顯好轉，大致維持獲利預估:  
展望 2027 年 ， 根據研調統計 ， 開始營運投片的新Fab 數量將自 2026
年的 13 座大幅增加至 2027 年的 23 座 ， 且成熟製程庫存健康化 ，電
源管理、車用與工業恢復，公司預期 2027 年矽晶圓供需環境將比
2026 年更吃緊。就環球晶而言，自 1Q27 起隨著新產能開出，且開
始適用調漲後的新 LTA 價格，將有助於營收及獲利轉強。此外，隨
著 AI 資料中心發展 ，SiC 市場正步入新成長週期 ，12 吋 SOI 晶圓也
受惠矽光子強勁動能推升需求，密蘇里州廠是目前美國唯一具備12
吋 SOI 製造能力的基地，產能持續爬升，客戶意向及預付款已可涵
蓋擬新增產能的 90%以上。基於以上，環球晶將步入產業上升循環，
預估 2027 年營收+33.8%YoY，稅後 EPS 33.14 元(原估 32.53 元)。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

한화에어로스페이스 (012450)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 방위산업 | 2026. 11. 2  
방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감

방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감 
지상방산 매출 1조 2,450억원으로 전년비 41%, 영업이익 217억원으로 전년비 
96.2% 급감하며 영업이익률은 1.7% 기록. 실적 급감에 대해 사측은 올해 주요 사업
의 납품 일정이 4분기에 집중됨에 따라 3분기 인도 물량이 적었던 것과 대전 사업장 
생산 중단에 따른 영향 때문이라고 설명. 단, 4분기에는 당초 계획에 따라 주요 사업의 
납품 물량이 집중되어 있어 실적이 개선될 것이고 대전 사업장은 내년 상반기 내에 정
상 가돌 하는 것이 목표라고 밝힘 
수주잔고 증가세 지속으로 성장 지속 가능할 것 
3분기 말 수주잔고 35조원으로 전년비 12.9% 증가. 이를 통한 지상방산 부문 성장 
지속 가능할 것으로 판단. 참고로 사측은 내년 매출 15% 수준 성장 전망 유지. 하지
만 금리 상승에 따른 글로벌 업종 평균 밸류에이션 하락에 따른 방산 부문 적용 멀티
플 하향 조정 그리고 상장 연결회사(한화오션, 한화시스템, 쎄트렉아이)와 상장 비연결
회사(한국항공우주)의 주가 하락 반영 영향으로 목표주가는 하향 조정 
8월 미 육군 차륜형 자주포 사업에서 시제 사업자로 선정. 향후 K9MH는 미 육군의 
작전 운용 개념에 맞춰 맞춤 조정을 거친 후에 특별한 결함 혹은 결격 사유가 없으면 
최종 계약 체결 예정. 동시에 미 육군의 궤도형 자주포 사업에도 참여할 계획. 뿐만 아
니라 스페인 K9, 크로아티아 천무 등으로 신시장 개척 역시 지속되고 있다는 점도 긍
정적 
 Analyst 이재광 
rheejaekwang @lssec.co.kr

Document 257: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29

Status: ok

### Page 1

(329180) 
HD 현대중공업 
2026.10.29

AI로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 610,000원(하향) 
 현재주가 285,500원(10/28)

 3Q26 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2%yoy), 영업이익은 8,992억원
(+61.3%yoy, OPM 15.6%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센서스 9,792억원 하회.  
 평균 환율이 약 79원 하락하며 영업이익 약 450억원이 줄었고, 조업
일수는 47일로 통상 계절성 대비 4일 추가 감소해 약 500억원의 이
익 감소 효과 발생. 임단협 추가 비용의 약 75%도 3분기에 선반영
(잔여분은 4분기 반영). 일회성 요인을 제외하면  이익 체력은 견조. 
 동사의 조선 부문 매출액은 4조 3,988억원(+44.0%yoy), 영업이익은 
6,463억원(+62.8%yoy, OPM 14.7%)을 기록. 상선 OPM은 14.7%로 
2Q26(17.8%) 대비 하락했으나 대부분 외생변수 영향. 3Q26 대형선 매출 
중 2023년 수주분 비중은 45%, 2024~2025년 수주분은 51%. 고선가 
물량의 매출 비중이 높아지는 2027년까지 수익성 개선 여력은 충분.  
 9월 말 누적 상선 수주는 138.0억달러로 연간 목표의 120%, 엔진기계는 
41.4억달러로 155%를 달성했으나 함정(13%)과 해양(5%)은 부진. LNG선
은 중국 조선소의 저가 수주와 발주 둔화로 선가가 일부 하락. 선주들이 현 
선가를 저점으로 인식하며 발주 협의를 재개하는 분위기. 북미 LNG 프로젝
트 FID가 이어지고 있어 2027년 LNG선 발주 회복 기대. 
 함정 부문은 매출 2,570억원(-14.6%qoq)으로 줄었으나, 수출 비중 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선으로 영업이익 386억원(+114%qoq, OPM 15.0%)을 기록. 
해양플랜트는 매출액 3,799억원(+35.3%yoy), 영업이익 1,265억원(OPM 
33.3%). C/O 180억원을 제외해도 Ruya 공정 본격화와 Trion 고수익 물량
으로 높은 수익성 유지. 2027년 일부 프로젝트 인도로 매출 감소가 예상되
나, LNG선·블록 물량 투입으로 고정비 부담을 완화할 계획. 신규 해상변전
소(OSS) 계약을 앞두고 있으며, 2027년 초 추가 수주 발표도 기대. 
 주목할 부분은 엔진기계. 영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)으로 높은 D/F 
엔진 비중을 바탕으로 견조한 수익성 유지. 육상발전용 힘센엔진은 올해   
3GW를 확보하며 2029년까지 계획 캐파 수준의 물량을 이미 채움. 생산 
예상 물량은 2028년 약 1.5GW, 2029년 약 3GW이며, 신규 공장 구축 이
후 연간 생산능력은 약 4GW로 확대 예정. 북미 Hyperscaler와의 직접 협
상이 늘고 있으며, 힘센엔진을 활용한 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)로 FDC 시장
까지 대응 가능. AI 데이터센터 전력 수요가 2028년 이후 새로운 이익 축. 
 미국에서는 Kiewit과 합작법인 설립 계약 체결. 군수지원함 등 지원함 사업
부터 추진하며, 현지 조선소 건설 과정에서 HII에 선박 블록을 공급하는 방
안도 협의 중. 구체적인 투자계획은 2026년 말까지 확정 목표. 국내에서는 
핵추진잠수함 초기 예산이 2027년 반영되며 동사도 사업 참여를 준비 중. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 61만원을 제시. 고선가 물량 매출 확
대와 육상발전용 엔진 성장으로 피크아웃을 이겨낼 굴지의 조선사.

시가총액(십억원) 29,966 
 발행주식수(천주) 104,961

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

Document 272: AI基建融资新裂痕！一批数据中心贷款被打包甩卖，星际之门项目也在其中

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI基建融资新裂痕！一批数据中心贷款被打包甩卖，星际之门项目也在其中.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-07
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

荷兰国际集团 ING 正寻求⼀次性抛售约 5 亿美元数据中⼼贷款组合，其中包括与 OpenAI 、
软银、甲⻣⽂联合推进的 " 星际之⻔ "AI 旗舰项⽬相关融资。更令市场警觉的是，甲⻣⽂已向
该项⽬新墨西哥州开发商发出不可抗⼒通知，暗⽰核⼼参与⽅正为退出预留法律空间。叠
加美国⼤选后科技股重挫、标普 500 蒸发逾 3 万亿美元的双重压⼒，此次抛售或成本轮 AI 基
建融资泡沫的最新裂缝。
摘要：
荷兰国际集团 ING 正寻求⼀次性出售约 5 亿美元的数据中⼼贷款组合，其中包括与 OpenAI 、软银
及甲⻣⽂联合推进的 " 星际之⻔ " （ Stargate ） AI 基础设施项⽬相关的融资，此举令本已承压的 AI
基建融资市场再添变数。
据彭博周六报道， ING 通过竞价出售流程（ bids-wanted-in-competition ）挂牌约 12 笔贷款，出
价截⽌⽇期为 11 ⽉ 19 ⽇，单笔出价⻔槛不低于 1000 万美元。
此次抛售发⽣在美国中期选举⺠主党⼤获全胜之后 —— 市场普遍担忧新政治格局将带来更严格

的科技监管，芯⽚股及新兴云计算公司遭到重创，标普 500 成分股市值合计蒸发逾 3 万亿美元。
此次出售的贷款组合中，⼀笔 2500 万美元的融资尤为引⼈关注 —— 该笔贷款与新墨西哥州⼀处
⼤型数据中⼼挂钩，该项⽬正是星际之⻔计划的重要组成部分。甲⻣⽂已就该新墨西哥州项⽬
向开发商发出不可抗⼒通知，意味着甲⻣⽂正在为可能退出相关义务预留法律空间。
星际之⻔项⽬现裂痕，不可抗⼒通知引发警觉
此次贷款出售中最受关注的细节，是甲⻣⽂向星际之⻔新墨西哥州项⽬开发商发出的不可抗⼒
通知。不可抗⼒条款通常是合同⼀⽅为摆脱既有承诺⽽启动的法律程序，意味着该⽅正在主
张 " 不可控事件 " 可免除其合同义务。
星际之⻔是美国规模最⼤的 AI 基础设施旗舰项⽬之⼀，由 OpenAI 、软银及甲⻣⽂联合推进。甲
⻣⽂在该项⽬中发出此类通知，意味着核⼼参与⽅正在重新评估项⽬推进的可⾏性，这对整个
AI 基建融资⽣态的信⼼构成直接冲击。
组合涵盖多家数据中⼼运营商，市场深度受考验
据彭博报道， ING 此次挂牌的贷款组合除星际之⻔相关融资外，还涵盖 Vantage Data Centers

及 CyrusOne 等数据中⼼运营商的债务。
数据中⼼融资在 AI 热潮期间⼀度是信贷市场最炙⼿可热的资产类别之⼀，相关贷款被打包出售给
养⽼基⾦等追求稳定收益的机构投资者。此次 5 亿美元的⼤规模集中抛售，且卖家选择匿名操
作，将直接检验在政治环境⽣变、股市⼤幅回调的双重压⼒下，这⼀市场的真实承接能⼒。
AI 基建股全线承压，融资成本上⾏⻛险浮现
此次贷款出售对权益市场的传导效应已有所显现。甲⻣⽂作为被直接点名的当事⽅，与英伟
达、微软、 CoreWeave ，以及数据中⼼地产运营商 Digital Realty 和 Equinix ⼀同承压。数据中⼼
运营商 Applied Digital 、 Cipher Digital 及 IREN 本周跌幅均超过 14% 。

Philippines: GDP growth to rebound on
public spending and manufacturing strength
We expect GDP growth to recover to 3.5% year-on-year in the third
quarter. This will be driven by a gradual normalisation in
government spending and continued strength in the
manufacturing sector, which is also reﬂected in robust electronics

China: Trade to remain strong as inﬂation
edges lower
China will release October inﬂation and trade data. We expect CPI

inﬂation to edge down to 0.8% YoY, from 0.9% in September,
suggesting that consumer price pressures remain subdued amid
continued weakness in domestic demand. By contrast, PPI inﬂation
is expected to rise further to 5.3% YoY, from 5.0%, driven mainly by
elevated energy and commodity prices.
Export and import growth are expected to accelerate to 40.7% YoY
and 37.3% YoY, respectively amid resilient external demand for
technology-related and manufactured goods and increased

commodity imports. The trade surplus is expected to narrow
slightly to $131.2bn, from $133.5bn in September, as import
growth accelerates.
Taiwan: Technology demand supports strong
trade
Taiwan releases October trade data on Monday. We expect export
growth to moderate to 51.3% YoY, from 60.9% in September. Even
so, growth should remain exceptionally strong, supported by
demand for semiconductors and other AI-related products. Imports
are expected to accelerate to 69.2% YoY, from 51.7%, reﬂecting

strong demand for semiconductor equipment, components and
advanced materials. Despite faster import growth, the trade surplus
is forecast to widen to $25.6bn, from $23.6bn in September,
highlighting the continued strength of Taiwan’s technology export
cycle.

Japan: Household spending, current account
and producer prices in focus
Japan will release September household spending and current
account data. The market expects household spending to remain
weak, falling 3.1% YoY, unchanged from August, highlighting
continued pressure on domestic consumption. The current account
surplus is forecast to narrow to JPY 3262bn, from JPY 4062bn.
Japan’s October PPI will be released on Thursday. Market consensus
expects producer price inﬂation to moderate to 6.5% YoY, from

6.8% in September. Prices are forecast to rise 0.2% month-on-
month following a 0.3% decline. This suggests that upstream price
pressures are gradually easing, though they remain elevated,
keeping expectations for further BoJ policy normalisation ﬁrmly in
focus.

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX tăng điểm trong tuần qua, +68.24 điểm tương ứng +4.00%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Thiết bị điện, Khách sạn - Nhà hàng và Thiết bị dịch vụ y tế là các nhóm ngành có 
mức tăng rõ rệt nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần 
trước với giá trị -966 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh duy 
trì mua ròng ở mức 137 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,335 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -3,049 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
1,815 hợp đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,000 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam hồi phục trở lại sau bốn tuần giảm liên tiếp, khi VN-Index bật tăng mạnh 
từ vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1.700 điểm với thanh khoản cải thiện. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh kéo dài 
khiến mặt bằng định giá về vùng hấp dẫn, lực cầu bắt đáy đã nhập cuộc quyết liệt hơn, 
với phiên 21/10 tăng gần 47 điểm — một trong những phiên tăng mạnh nhất nhiều tháng 
gần đây. Điểm tích cực và đáng chú ý nhất là khối ngoại đã xuất hiện hai phiên đảo chiều 
mua ròng trở lại sau khoảng 20 phiên bán ròng liên tiếp, tập trung vào nhóm Bluechip 
như VIC, TCB, VHM, MSN — tín hiệu đầu tiên cho thấy áp lực rút vốn ngoại có thể đang dần 
hạ nhiệt. Thông tin tích cực tác động lên thị trường đến từ các chỉ đạo của NHNN và Chính 
phủ về việc hạ mặt bằng lãi suất để hỗ trợ tăng trưởng kinh tế. Bên cạnh đó, mùa báo cáo 
KQKD quý 3 khả quan đang diễn ra cũng là yếu tố hỗ trợ xu hướng thị trường chung.

Trung tâm phân tích 
(+84) 24-7303-5333  kbsv.research@kbsec.com.vn

19/10/2026

Nguồn: V_KBSource

Triển vọng kinh tế 4Q2026 
Giữ nhịp tăng trưởng, vững vàng về itch

Một số dự báo chính của KBSV về kinh tế Việt Nam trong năm 2026:

1) Tăng trưởng GDP năm 2026 được dự báo sẽ đạt 9.0%, với động lực chính 
đến từ định hướng đẩy mạnh giải ngân đầu tư công của Chính phủ, dòng 
vốn FDI duy trì tích cực và hoạt động sản xuất công nghiệp phục hồi theo 
xuất khẩu. Về rủi ro, chúng tôi cho rằng Việt Nam sẽ tiếp tục đối mặt với 
một số yếu tố bất định từ bên ngoài, bao gồm: rủi ro xung đột Mỹ – Iran 
kéo dài hơn dự kiến, xu hướng tăng của mặt bằng lãi suất toàn cầu gây gia 
tăng áp lực tài chính và kéo giảm tổng cầu, cùng nguy cơ vỡ bong bóng AI.

2) Chúng tôi nâng dự báo lạm phát bình quân năm 2026 của Việt Nam lên 
mức 4.7% YoY, cao hơn mục tiêu kiểm soát lạm phát của Chính phủ, dựa 
trên kịch bản dự báo thận trọng hơn về diễn biến xung đột địa chính trị và 
giá năng lượng thế giới trong ngắn hạn.

3) Mặt bằng lãi suất trong quý IV được dự báo sẽ ổn định quanh vùng hiện 
tại và không xuất hiện thêm nhịp tăng đột biến, nhờ dòng tiền cải thiện 
từ giải ngân đầu tư công và kỳ vọng có thêm các biện pháp hỗ trợ thanh 
khoản trung dài hạn từ phía Chính phủ và NHNN.

4) Tỷ giá USD/VND trong quý cuối năm có thể chịu một số sức ép do Fed dự 
báo còn thêm một lần tăng lãi suất nữa trong năm. Dù vậy, chúng tôi cho 
rằng tỷ giá USD/VND khó tăng mạnh mà chỉ quay lại mức đầu năm, dựa 
trên kỳ vọng chỉ số DXY không biến động mạnh, nguồn cung ngoại tệ cải 
thiện (FDI giải ngân, kiều hối, nâng hạng TTCK) và chênh lệch lãi suất 
VND–USD duy trì trạng thái dương.

Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

### Page 4

4

21/10/2026

CTCP VÀNG BẠC ĐÁ QUÝ PHÚ NHUẬN (PNJ)  
Cập nhật ĐHĐCĐ bất thường 21/10/2026

Tái cơ cấu để xử lý áp lực thanh khoản  
— ĐHĐCĐ bất thường PNJ ngày 21/10/2026 đã thông qua điều chỉnh kế hoạch kinh 
doanh và phương án phát hành riêng lẻ để bổ sung vốn tái cấu trúc. Ban lãnh đạo đặt 
ra các vấn đề trọng yếu là đáp ứng nhu cầu thanh khoản và chuẩn bị nguồn lực để thực 
hiện các cam kết thu đổi đã tích lũy trong quá khứ. Bên cạnh đó, các cổ đông tham dự 
cũng đặt các câu hỏi liên quan đến tình hình hoạt động qua các điểm chính (1) dòng 
tiền hoạt động kinh doanh; (2) diễn biến thị trường sau khi PNJ áp dụng chính sách thu 
đổi mới; (3) hiệu quả hoạt động sau tái cấu trúc.

Nội dung chính tại đại hội 
— Theo Ban lãnh đạo, thông tin liên quan đến P-Lab từ đầu tháng 7 khiến khách hàng gia 
tăng bán lại sản phẩm. Tổng giá trị thu đổi và mua lại trong tháng đạt gần 6,000 tỷ VND, 
so với khoảng 7,000 tỷ VND trong cả năm 2025. Theo BCTC riêng, nguồn tiền khả dụng 
giảm từ khoảng 3,700 tỷ VND cuối Q2/2026 xuống 770 tỷ VND cuối Q3/2026, đã bao 
gồm khoản vay 700 tỷ VND từ Chủ tịch HĐQT. Tỷ trọng kim cương và trang sức kim 
cương trong tổng tồn kho tăng từ 41% lên 52%. 
— Q3/2026 ghi nhận lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND trước phần tăng trích lập dự phòng. Để giảm 
áp lực thanh toán, PNJ kéo dài thời gian chi trả đối với kim cương lên 120 ngày từ tháng 
07/2026. Thay đổi chính sách cũng ảnh hưởng đến niềm tin khách hàng và sức mua, cụ 
thể doanh thu và lợi nhuận gộp Q3/2026 đạt lần lượt 6,995/609 tỷ VND, giảm 14%/62% 
YoY. Chi phí bán hàng và quản lý giảm 13% YoY chưa đủ bù đắp mức giảm lợi nhuận 
gộp, khiến PNJ lỗ sau thuế 258 tỷ VND, so với lãi 496 tỷ VND trong Q3/2025. 
— PNJ nâng giả định tỷ lệ thu đổi trên giá trị thu đổi còn lại lên 80%, so với 28.8% đối với 
nhóm sản phẩm giai đoạn 2020–2026 trong ước tính tại BCTC bán niên 2026. Tổng dự 
phòng tổn thất dự kiến tăng từ 2,267 tỷ VND lên 7,071 tỷ VND. Kế hoạch điều chỉnh ghi 
nhận doanh thu 39,057 tỷ VND, lỗ trước thuế 6,059 tỷ VND và lỗ sau thuế 6,271 tỷ VND. 
Theo Ban lãnh đạo, LNST loại trừ dự phòng ước khoảng 800 tỷ VND, chủ yếu nhờ kết 
quả Q1/2026. Khoản dự phòng phản ánh tổn thất ước tính cho toàn bộ kịch bản, không 
đồng nghĩa toàn bộ giá trị sẽ được chi trả ngay trong kỳ. 
— Giá trị cam kết thu đổi tích lũy đối với kim cương và trang sức kim cương tại cuối 
Q3/2026 được PNJ ước tính khoảng 21,000 tỷ VND. Với hàng đã bán, doanh nghiệp dự 
kiến chuyển đổi quyền lợi khách hàng sang sản phẩm và các chương trình ưu đãi. Với 
sản phẩm bán mới, chính sách thu đổi định hướng theo giá thị trường tại thời điểm 
giao dịch, thay cho tỷ lệ cố định trên giá trị hóa đơn, nhằm hạn chế tiếp tục tích lũy 
nghĩa vụ tiềm tàng trong tương lai theo cơ chế cũ. 
Tham khảo nội dung chi tiết trong báo cáo tại đây.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến xanh thân dài trên khung tuần và đáng chú ý là cho phản ứng 
bật tăng ngay tại cạnh dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn, cho thấy tín hiệu 
tích cực đã quay trở lại với xu hướng ngắn hạn. Trên khung ngày, sau 2 phiên điều chỉnh, 
rung lắc vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng đảo chiều tăng điểm khá dốc, với 3 cây nến xanh liên 
tiếp với thanh khoản được tăng cường, củng cố thêm tín hiệu xác nhận cho đà hồi phục. 
Sau nhịp bật tăng mạnh từ đáy, VNINDEX được dự báo sẽ sớm gặp phải áp lực điều chỉnh 
khi quay lên điểm giữa của dải đi ngang theo mẫu hình tam giác, tương ứng vùng kháng cự 
kế tiếp tại quanh 1810. Tuy nhiên, xu hướng ngắn hạn vẫn có phần nghiêng về chiều tăng, 
với tín hiệu xác nhận đáy của một số mã cổ phiếu sau quá trình sụt giảm kéo dài.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

2 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Stronger Growth, Costlier Money
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
The latest GDP report and annual revisions show a sturdier U.S. economy than previously
reported. According to the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA), real GDP grew at a 2.2%
annualized pace in Q2 compared to 1.5% previously estimated, and Q1 was revised
from 2.1% to 2.5%. Both 2024 and 2025 were also revised slightly higher. Most of the

first half of the year revision was in categories related to the Artificial Intelligence (AI)
buildout, but real consumer spending has also been revised slightly higher, accelerating
from 0.7% annualized growth in Q1 to 3.8% in Q2 despite surging energy prices and
slow hiring.
Spending in July and August remained much stronger than labor market conditions alone
would suggest, also averaging 3.7% annualized growth. Upside revisions to net interest

income and personal saving suggest households have benefited from more income than
was previously apparent, with personal saving revised higher back to 2023. The new data,
according to the BEA, show the January to July 2026 savings rate averaged 4.8% versus
3.3% prerevision, strengthening consumer spending underpinnings despite moderate
hiring, high energy prices and rising borrowing costs.
The BEA update also included inflation methodological changes related to categories

inflated by the AI boom—such as portfolio management fees and computer software and
accessories. These improved the inflation picture to some extent—August “core” PCE
inflation was reduced by 0.3 ppts to 3% YoY, bringing it closer to “core” consumer price
index inflation, which was 2.5% YoY and 2% on a three-month annualized basis. While still
above the 2% Federal Reserve (Fed) target, “core” PCE now appears stable at 3% year over
year since January rather than accelerating. These methodological changes are expected

to dampen pass-through of AI-related price pressures into “core” inflation and effects of
stock-market swings on inflation.
They also produced an encouraging improvement in recent PCE inflation momentum.
Three-month annualized “core” PCE inflation slowed from 4.3% at the peak of tariff
effects in February to 2.1% in August. For durable goods, this rate was down from 5.8%
in February to 1.5% in August, and, for services ex-energy and healthcare, it slowed from

4.2% to 2.2%, for example. Inflation is volatile on a three-month change basis, so it
remains to be seen whether this moderation continues without further Fed tightening.
For now, the trends in the San Francisco Fed’s cyclical versus acyclical inflation measures
diverged again in August. As expected, given softening wage growth, prices most sensitive
to the labor market have eased further. At 3% YoY, this inflation category is down from
8% in early 2023 and 4.6% two years ago is back to prepandemic levels and is likely

to ease more (Exhibit 1A). In contrast, supply bottlenecks, high energy prices and other
industry-specific factors have kept acyclical inflation on an uptrend. As shown in Exhibit
1B, the relentless increase in freight costs could prolong upside pressure on this category.
The diesel market has tightened in recent months, boosting its price by 70% YoY,
consistent with Brent oil at around $200 per barrel and large reverberations across
a wide swath of goods and services prices. A government-policy-related decline in

available truck drivers is also raising transportation costs more than expected. So are
tight ocean shipping capacity and longer/more expensive voyages due to the U.S./Iran
War. Supply-chain stress also increased as companies rushed to front-run potential new
tariff increases.
On the positive side for the inflation outlook, unit labor costs (ULC) are up modestly
as robust productivity growth has absorbed much of the wage growth, limiting a major
source of domestic inflation. Upside productivity growth revisions are likely to further

reduce ULC, which could help shift focus from energy and AI-related inflation to
eventual disinflationary effects once oil and refined fuel prices start to ease. Sustained
productivity gains would support growth and margins while limiting inflation and the
need for much higher interest rates.
Investment Implications
We favor selective Equity exposure
to companies with durable demand
and pricing power, while remaining
cautious about businesses most

exposed to fuel, freight, and
financing costs. We would also
gradually increase long-duration
exposure.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
Surging nominal growth has boosted corporate revenues (up 13% YoY in Q2, according to the
BEA) while contained unit labor costs helped raise margins and profits more than expected.
With labor costs declining as a share of revenues, investment-related tax incentives and
rock-bottom net interest expense, pre-tax nonfinancial corporate profits as measured in GDP
accounts rose 21% YoY in Q2. Total corporate profits after-tax were up 18.2% YoY, according to

the BEA.
Surging AI-related imports and investment are keeping the business and profits cycle on
an uptrend both here and abroad. According to BofA Global Research, the global economic
cycle backdrop improved further in September, as reflected in a broad-based increase in its
Global Wave1 indicator. Broadening U.S. manufacturing strength, fueled by ongoing AI-driven
investment, a healthier orders backlog and low inventories suggest that the expansion has

room to run—a constructive environment for earnings. Typically, a Global Wave upswing is
consistent with rising earnings revisions ratios and equity price uptrends. According to the
Global Wave, global earnings growth expectations remain exceptionally strong.
Still, absent a resolution of war-related energy and transportation disruptions, the same
industrial strength intensifies inflation pressures. A prolonged supply shock raises the risk of
restrictive interest rates and weakening economic growth. In this context, the surge in the

10-year Treasury yield above 5% is starting to reflect more than optimism about U.S. growth
and strengthening productivity outlook. Oil’s tightest recent correlation with 10-year Treasury
yields in 35 years suggests a common energy-price thread behind rising yields in developed
countries.
Bond market spillovers from normalizing Japan yields and capital repatriation are another
common thread. Higher yields on foreign sovereign bonds such as in Japan—which has

escaped stagnation and deflation by intensely pursuing investment-led growth—weaken the
relative appeal of Treasurys. That distinction matters for portfolios. A yield increase driven
mainly by durable productivity gains would come with a stronger outlook for future income
and earnings. A yield increase driven by cost shocks and greater uncertainty raises financing
costs without the same benefit.
In sum, the economy remains more resilient than expected, but the bond market is also

charging more for an uncertain inflation outlook given untethered government debt and
cooling global demand for Treasurys spurred by geopolitical tensions and Japan capital
repatriation. The case for much further tightening becomes stronger if wage growth
strengthens, productivity lags, and unit labor costs reaccelerate, causing cyclical inflation to
also pick up steam. The case becomes weaker if that measure stays contained while fuel and
freight pressures recede or rising Treasury yields reduce asset prices and economic growth.

etc.).
B) Surge in U.S. AI-related imports, tariff front-running, shipping disruptions and fuel- price
spike feed acyclical inflation.

4 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Yields, Yen and Iran: What Haunts the “House”
Lauren Sanfilippo, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
We recently highlighted the three-front challenge facing Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent:
engineering a stronger yen, containing the 10-year Treasury yield and securing a diplomatic
resolution to the conflict with Iran.

Bessent recently has delivered one of the year’s more memorable lines while discussing
Treasury policy and the yen: “I have asymmetric information. I am the house now.” 2 Yet several
challenges remain: the 10-year Treasury yield is near levels last seen in the early 2000s, the
yen remains structurally weaker, and a phased agreement to reopen the Strait of Hormuz
appears remote. The resulting strain on energy markets keeps inflation risks elevated.

Put differently, oil prices, Treasury yields and the dollar-yen exchange rate are all trending
against Bessent’s objectives. Below, we examine how each variable is haunting his “house.”
Haunting the “House” is the Yen. Since the U.S. and Japan mounted a record $98.7B
coordinated intervention in late July after the yen weakened to a 40-year low near 164 per
dollar, the currency has appreciated roughly 4% against the dollar. The move was further
supported on September 18 when the BoJ raised rates by 25 basis points (bps) to 1.25%,

their highest level since the mid-1990s (Exhibit 2A). However, the split 7–2 vote and Governor
Ueda’s limited forward guidance leave the path of further tightening uncertain. The key
question is whether additional BoJ hikes can produce sustained appreciation in the yen, or
merely reinforce the temporary relief provided by the summer intervention.
Bessent, meanwhile, has repeatedly signaled his preference for a stronger yen. Partly because
yen stability is also a U.S. financing concern. Persistent currency weakness could prompt

Japan to support its currency by selling foreign assets, including U.S. Treasurys—which would
be an unwelcome prospect as Washington partially relies on foreign capital flows to fund its
large fiscal deficits. That potential “haunts” the Treasury Secretary.  At precisely the moment
Washington needs foreign capital to finance its enormous fiscal deficits, it would be inopportune
for one of America’s largest creditors to become a significant seller of Treasury securities.

Haunting the “House” are Yields. The surge in yields runs counter to a White House intent
on lowering borrowing costs. The benchmark 10-year yield has broken above the 5% threshold
and climbed to levels not seen since 2007. At around 5.30%, it stands more than a full
percentage point above where it began the year (Exhibit 2B). The level is striking but so is the
speed of the move: Stronger-than-expected economic data, including a survey showing U.S.

2 September 8, 2026.

complicating the macro backdrop

purchasers grew at 4.6% for Q2) and

that the fundamental backdrop for
U.S. Equities remains constructive.

business activity expanded at the fastest pace in more than five years, helped trigger an 18
bps surge in the 10-year yield—among its largest daily moves in decades. The 5-year yield also
topped 5% for the first time since 2007, while the 30-year yield reached 5.63%, its highest
level since 2002.
Compounding the move is inflation, running above the Fed’s 2% target for 67 months,

large fiscal deficits and the prospect of continued growth in Treasury issuance. Against this
backdrop, Treasury bought back $4.1 billion of long-dated debt, versus a maximum of $6
billion. That effort has done little to arrest the selloff. A subsequent auction priced nearly $4
billion of 7-year notes at 5.085%, the highest yield for that maturity in 33 years.
All in, the MOVE index, a key gauge of Treasury market volatility, jumped from 78 to 95 on

Wednesday September 23 and then to 104 the following day, its highest level since the early
days of the Iran war (Exhibit 3A).
Haunting the “House” is the Iran conflict. Early expectations that military pressure and
economic sanctions would quickly bring Iran to the negotiating table have yet to materialize.
Iranian officials have signaled that a full reopening of the Strait of Hormuz remains unlikely
while sanctions stay in place. Although talks between Washington and Tehran are continuing,

conflicting signals and lingering skepticism from both sides have kept tensions elevated.
As a result, oil prices have remained elevated in a range even as some Middle East supply
disruptions began to ease.
Startling is that Brent crude is up more than 60% this year, but refined product prices
have risen even faster. Ongoing disruptions to energy flows through the Strait of Hormuz,
combined with Ukrainian strikes on Russian refining infrastructure have tightened global

supplies of crude oil and refined fuels. Saudi Arabia has restored some drone-disrupted flows,
providing roughly 3.5 million barrels a day of relief (roughly 3% of global consumption).
The squeeze is mostly acute in diesel markets, where inventories remain constrained and
prices continue to climb (Exhibit 3B). U.S. diesel prices recently surpassed $6.50 per gallon,
the highest level on record, just as Russia has extended restrictions on diesel exports into

October. Against this backdrop, proposals to ban U.S. fuel exports risks worsening the
imbalance. According to S&P Global, an export ban could reduce refinery runs by nearly 2
million barrels per day and significantly cut gasoline production, potentially driving gasoline
and jet fuel prices even higher. As long as tensions persist, the oil impulse remains a key
impediment to structurally lower inflation.
Investment implications. Treasury Secretary Bessent increasingly sits at the intersection of

monetary policy, fiscal policy, foreign policy and national security. We remain constructive on
the U.S. economy and overweight U.S. Equities but are watching as these fronts challenge the
narrative. There is a great deal riding on the “house” versus the key macro variables of today.
Exhibit 3: Pulse Check on: Equity/Bond Volatility and Diesel/Gasoline Prices.
A) Bond and Equity Market Volatility Diverge. B) Pain at the Pump: Diesel Prices Surge Ahead of Gasoline.

6 of 8 October 5, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
When It Comes to Attracting Foreign Capital, the U.S. Remains Exceptional
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
There may be no better vote of confidence in a country than the willingness of
foreigners to put their money there. And by that measurement, the U.S. remains the
exception—both portfolio and FDI inflows are currently running at record highs based

on the latest data. This dynamic is one key reason why we remain constructive on U.S.
assets over the longer term.
Notwithstanding all the handwringing about America’s fiscal deficits, tariffs and rising
government debt, global investors sunk some $942B in net purchases of U.S. Equities in
the 12 months to July, the highest rolling 12-month total on record. In Q2, net purchase
of U.S. Equities soared to $426B, up 62% from the same period a year ago (Exhibit 4A).

By country, flows were down from China—not surprisingly—but strong from Taiwan and
South Korea, who have taken to recycling their massive AI-generated trade surplus back
into the U.S. In the end, strong portfolio inflows suggest that international investors
remain willing to increase their exposure to the world's largest capital market—that’s
bullish for U.S. assets.
Meanwhile, Exhibit 4B highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be

inside the U.S., with FDI inflows totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year.
That is a staggering 103% jump from a year ago and means FDI inflows are on track for
a record year, estimated at $480B for 2026.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Two, within the $32 trillion-plus economy with extraordinarily consumer

purchasing power, foreign firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment
than just trade (exports). Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers,
semiconductors, power generation, etc., has created numerous potential investment
opportunities for many foreign firms that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging
capex cycle. Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly
developed technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.

In the end, double-barreled capital inflows speak to the absolute and relative
attractiveness of the U.S. economy and capital markets. Stay invested in America—the
rest of the world is.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

ICE BofA MOVE Index is a market-implied measure of U.S. bond market volatility.
Consumer Price Index measures the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a market basket of consumer goods and services.
Baltic Dry Index is a daily shipping and trade index published by the Baltic Exchange in London that measures the cost of moving raw materials like iron ore, coal, and grain by sea.
Volatility Index (VIX) is a real-time market indicator that measures the stock market's expected volatility over the next 30 days.

or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: whiplash
By Ryan Wells & Elliot Clarke
11:20 November 06 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, the RBA delivered another 25bp rate increase at its November meeting, taking the cash
rate to 4.85%. The move was widely expected, reflecting the Board’s continued concern around
inflation following the recent run higher in global energy prices, firmer domestic capacity pressures and

signs that inflation expectations have edged up. Inflation is now not expected to return to the mid-point
of the target band until mid-2028. This is despite weaker growth expectations and the unemployment

### Page 2

rate trending up to 5.1% by end-2028 on the RBA’s forecasts.
Importantly, though, the decision was not unanimous, with three members preferring to remain on hold
given downside risks to activity and the restraining influence of slower growth. Governor Bullock’s
suggestion that it was time to “take stock” after five rate increases also struck a less hawkish note,
while the statement dropped September’s explicit reference to raising rates further “if needed”. In our

view, February represents the next plausible opportunity for another hike, but we see this only as a risk.
Policy’s effect on housing is becoming increasingly visible. Cotality home prices fell a further 1.2% in
October to now be 6.7% below March’s peak. The cumulative price decline is approaching the scale of
previous housing corrections but appears to have regained momentum after September’s rate
increase. Dwelling approvals saw a 5.1% headline gain in September, but this was driven by volatile

unit approvals while detached house approvals fell sharply, suggesting higher rates and the
established-market downturn are beginning to weigh on the construction pipeline.
Official data on nominal household spending meanwhile rose 0.6% in September and 2.1% for Q3;
however, much of the recent acceleration looks to be price-driven, particularly for fuel. Real spending
volumes expanded by a much more modest (but still solid) 0.7% in Q3.
The Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel provides a granular look at income, spending and saving flows.

The latest update suggests that, beneath the resilient headline story, households are increasingly being
squeezed, particularly those with low savings buffers. With another rate increase now delivered,
housing wealth declining and sentiment weak, Q3 increasingly looks like the high-water mark for
growth in consumer spending.
Lastly for Australia, in September the goods trade surplus widened sharply to $2.8bn, with non-
monetary gold trade yet again adding considerable noise month-to-month. The most notable

development was another exceptionally strong result for data-processing equipment imports, taking the
Q3 total to $11.1bn. This is another clear marker of the scale of Australia’s data-centre investment
boom, which should feature prominently in both business investment and imports in the Q3 National
Accounts.
Offshore, the world has been focused on the US mid-term elections. In the House, Senate and a series
of State Governor races, the Democrats experienced resounding victory. Come 2027, the Democrats

will have a comfortable majority in both houses of Congress and are expected to make use of this
strength to respond to voter concerns, particularly over cost-of-living pressures. The election result also
limits President Trump’s ability to fund an extended military action against Iran. Notably, both
immediately before and after the election, President Trump and the administration have exhibited a
greater interest in furthering negotiations with Iran. It is therefore not a surprise that Brent oil has

backed off its most recent highs around USD118 last Friday, although the scale of the drop to near
USD93 yesterday is arguably too optimistic. Brent has since traded back up to above USD99, still 16%
below Friday’s peak.
While the initial reaction to the US election result was a robust broad-based rally in major equity
indexes, concerns over the potential for tighter regulation of AI and the related infrastructure buildout

### Page 3

Related articles
jolted US equities lower overnight – the Nasdaq falling 5.5% and S&P500 4.4%, both a multiple of the
prior session’s gains. That said, US term interest rates and FOMC rate expectations have also been
scaled back; so, while tech may come under greater scrutiny, a foundation for better outcomes across
the rest of the US economy may become evident in time. US data out this week continued to suggest
their economy is in robust health overall. And, if tensions in the Middle East abate, the FOMC’s

concerns over US inflation should subside. Gaining enough comfort for the FOMC to not hike in
December is a high bar, but the probability of further hikes in 2027 should continue to fall.
Turning then to the UK, the Bank of England raised Bank Rate by 25bps to 4.00% at their November
meeting as expected. On the Bank's baseline expectation, UK inflation is forecast to rise from 3.5%yr
at September to 5.00% by mid-2027. Activity data has also surprised to the upside in recent months,

though "there remains limited evidence of knock-on effects on other prices and wages".
In the press conference, Governor Bailey recognised the nascent improvement in energy price
pressures over the past 48 hours and the effectiveness of monetary policy but also conveyed that
"Unless progress towards resolving the conflict is made, or there is other news which softens the risk to
inflationary pressures, a further increase in Bank Rate may be warranted". Governor Bailey's tone and

comments made in the minutes by other Committee members did not suggest this was a decision that
needs to be made in December, but rather a risk to be judged as additional information is received.
This points to further tightening being more likely in early 2027 than before, and only if conditions / risks
warrant.

Document 317: Could Midterms Give Wall Street A Cold Shower_

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Could Midterms Give Wall Street A Cold Shower?

Could Midterms Give Wall Street A Cold Shower?

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Could Midterms Give Wall Street
A Cold Shower?
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 2 Nov 2026
View in browser

I. When Gridlock Isn't Good for Stocks
The US midterm elections will be over tomorrow night. According to
betting markets, Democrats have roughly a 95% probability of winning the
House and a 76% probability of taking the Senate (charts).
We suspect that stock investors have yet to fully discount the possibility of
a Democratic sweep of Congress. Some may remain skeptical that polls
confirming strong support for Democrats are accurate. They might reckon

### Page 2

that Democrats are more likely than Republicans to talk to pollsters over
the phone.
In any event, Wall Street traditionally welcomes political gridlock because
it reduces the likelihood that Congress will pass extremist legislation. But
this time could be different for two reasons.
First, deepening partisanship could turn gridlock into damaging political
paralysis at a time when the Bond Vigilantes are already uneasy about
Washington's unsustainable fiscal trajectory. A Democratic sweep could

complicate negotiations over government spending and the debt ceiling.
Prolonged shutdowns and fiscal standoffs could push Treasury yields
higher, weighing on equity valuations.

### Page 3

Second, gridlock's traditional ability to constrain policymaking may not
hold this time. President Donald Trump has relied heavily on executive
authority, with some of his most market-disruptive policies, including tariffs
and military action against Iran, pursued largely without congressional
approval. A Democratic-controlled Congress may therefore do little to
restrain him. Moreover, with the midterms behind him, Trump may feel
even less constrained politically and more willing to pursue controversial

policies through executive action. Hence, a blue wave could deliver a cold
shower for stocks, though we would view a significant selloff as a buying
opportunity given the remarkable strength of the economy and corporate
earnings.
One encouraging development ahead of the elections was today's reports
that the US and Iran are working on a peaceful diplomatic solution to their
war. Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian said he has granted his
government greater authority to pursue peace talks, while President

Trump and Vice President Vance also signaled progress. Hopes for a deal
sent the price of Brent crude oil down 7.6% to $104.77 per barrel (chart).
However, we have been here before with apparent diplomatic
breakthroughs. We still see a high risk of military re-escalation. The bid-
ask spread remains wide, with Tehran demanding sanctions relief, the
release of frozen assets, and an end to the US naval blockade of Iranian
ports, while Washington insists on nuclear concessions. Meanwhile, Iran's

leverage over the Strait of Hormuz has been weakening. According to
shipping data, for roughly half of September, Gulf oil exporters shipped
more crude than they were shipping before the war.

### Page 4

II. US Economy Continues To Roar
The US economy remains strong despite the volatility of the domestic
political and geopolitical scenes. Consider the following:
(1) Construction spending. Construction spending fell 1.9% m/m in
September to $2.19 trillion (saar), reversing most of August's upwardly
revised 2.0% increase. Nevertheless, spending remains just below last
year's record high (chart).
September's decline was driven by a 5.5% m/m drop in private residential

construction, attributable to a 13.0% plunge in home improvements
(chart). Elevated materials costs and higher mortgage rates continue to
weigh on the housing sector. Meanwhile, private nonresidential
construction rose 1.1% m/m, supported by continued investment in data
centers and power infrastructure.

### Page 5

The AI investment boom remains a powerful tailwind. Data center
construction spending surged 8.6% m/m and 90% y/y in September,
reaching a record $96.5 billion annual rate (chart).
(2) Manufacturing. The ISM M-PMI rose from 54.5 in September to 55.5
in October, marking its tenth consecutive month of expansion, the longest
streak since 2022. Production jumped to the highest reading since
November 2021, while new orders edged up to 55.5. Employment also

remained in expansionary territory (chart).

### Page 6

Inflationary pressures remain troublesome. The prices-paid index eased
from 77.9 to 75.2 in October but remained above 70 for the ninth
consecutive month. Regional Fed surveys also point to persistent price
pressures. Higher energy costs, tariffs, and elevated metals prices
continue to boost input costs (chart).
(3) Bank lending. The Fed's latest Senior Loan Officer Opinion Survey
(SLOOS) painted a mixed picture of credit demand. Banks reported
weaker demand for commercial and industrial (C&I) loans, mortgages,

and auto loans in Q4, while credit-card demand strengthened (chart).

### Page 7

Lending standards remained broadly unchanged for C&I loans but
tightened somewhat for commercial real estate loans (chart).
Encouragingly, actual bank lending tells a happier story. C&I loans surged
11.3% y/y through October 21, while total loans and leases increased
7.9% y/y (chart).

### Page 8

Credit growth is broad across bank sizes, with lending up 8.1% y/y at
large banks and 5.4% at small banks (chart).
Meanwhile, banks' allowances for loan and lease losses have remained
relatively stable at around $202 billion, despite the acceleration in lending
(chart).

### Page 9

clause.
